    SELECT [n].[NhanVienId], [n].[ChucVuId], [n].[DiaChi], [n].[Email], [n].[GioiTinh], [n].[HoTen], [n].[LuongCoBan], [n].[NgaySinh], [n].[NgayVaoLam], [n].[PhongBanId], [n].[PhuCap], [n].[SoDienThoai], [n].[Thuong]

) AS [n0]
